# 2027 EE Internships & Co-ops — US & Canada

The premier student-maintained list of **electrical engineering** internships and co-ops in the **United States** and **Canada**. Updated hourly from company career pages.

Hardware · RF · Analog · Power · VLSI/ASIC · FPGA · PCB · Test · Photonics · Avionics

> Exclusive to EE internships/co-ops in the US, Canada, or Remote (US/Canada). Software-only and non-EE roles are not listed.

**739 open** · 1074 total · Summer 2027 (534 open) · Off-cycle & co-ops (205 open) · Updated September 21, 2026

Contribute via [issue](https://github.com/aprameyak/shabajoba/issues/new/choose) or PR — see [CONTRIBUTING.md](./CONTRIBUTING.md).

## FAQ

**Where do listings come from?**  
Direct company ATS boards (Greenhouse, Lever, Ashby, Workday, Oracle, iCIMS, and others), USAJOBS, hardware feeds, and community submissions — refreshed hourly.

**How do I know a role is still open?**  
Open roles have an Apply link. Closed roles show 🔒. Links are checked regularly.

**Visa / citizenship?**  
🛂 = no sponsorship · 🇺🇸 = U.S. citizenship required. If neither flag appears, check the posting — status may be unknown.

**Found a non-EE role or a mistake?**  
Open an issue or PR. This list stays EE-only on purpose.
